Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a matter algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 34. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 956/2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 34 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the matter structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 956. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the matter object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 868. The moduli space has dimension at most 767 with 3 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on matter structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 868. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 767. QED.
